**Look states for the sequence (bible §12; file 01 state table):** Tut B2 (jacket over tunic, L2), G1 glow (COMP), nape scar, red scarf bound round his chest 9.1–9.3 only, LEFT-wrist seam cracked from 09.18 (9.4), right-hand tremor, ceramic left foot, ebony stick right hand, dagger at belt, Rami's notebook. ## Scene 19 — EXT. GREAT ATEN TEMPLE, AKHETATEN - DAY (MEMORY) (SUPER AKHETATEN. c. 1336 BC.)
